                           ReturnReceiptLine_L.RESET;

                           ReturnReceiptLine_L.SETFILTER(Type,'<>%1',ReturnReceiptLine_L.Type::" ");

                             REPEAT

                                 IF SalesLine_L.GET(SalesLine_L."Document Type"::"Return Order",ReturnReceiptLine_L."Return Order No.",
                                                    ReturnReceiptLine_L."Return Order Line No.") THEN BEGIN
                                   // --> 20140407-APP20-CMC02.02.00-1939-FEC01
                                   {BEFORE
                                   IF (SalesLine_L."Price Anomaly Type" = SalesLine_L."Price Anomaly Type"::" ") OR
                                      (SalesLine_L."Anomaly Approved" = TRUE) THEN
                                     AllAnomalyLines_L := FALSE;
                                   AFTER}
                                   IF IsReturnSalesLineOK(SalesLine_L) THEN
                                     AllAnomalyLines_L := FALSE;
                                   // --< 20140407-APP20-CMC02.02.00-1939-FEC01
                                 END ELSE
                                   // --> 20130801-App20-CMC02.02.00-FEC02
                                   {BEFORE
                                   ERROR(Text8053025,FORMAT(ReturnReceiptLine_L."Return Order No."),FORMAT(ReturnReceiptLine_L."Return Order Line No."));
                                   AFTER}
                                   BEGIN END;
                                   // --< 20130801-App20-CMC02.02.00-FEC02
                               END; // 20140207-App20-CMC02.02.00-1684-OAR01
                             UNTIL (ReturnReceiptLine_L.NEXT = 0) OR NOT AllAnomalyLines_L;
                           IF AllAnomalyLines_L THEN
                             CurrReport.SKIP;
                           // <-- 20130604-App20-CMC02.02.00-FDA01

                             IF CampaignNo_L <> CampNo_G THEN
                               CurrReport.SKIP;
                           //<--20130701-App22-CMC02.02.00-AST01

                           //--> 20131129-App22-CMC02.02.00-538-FHU01
                           IF County_G <> '' THEN
                             IF County_L <> County_G THEN
                               CurrReport.SKIP;
                           //<-- 20131129-App22-CMC02.02.00-538-FHU01

                           IF DueDateInLine_G  AND ((L_InvoiceDueDate > G_EndingDueDateReq) OR (L_InvoiceDueDate = 0D)) THEN
                             CurrReport.SKIP;

                           IF "Sales Shipment Line".Type = "Sales Shipment Line".Type::" " THEN BEGIN
                             IF NOT G_CopyTextLines THEN
                               CurrReport.SKIP;
                           END;

                           // --> 20130604-App20-CMC02.02.00-FDA01
                           IF L_SalesLine.GET(L_SalesLine."Document Type"::Order,"Order No.","Order Line No.") THEN BEGIN
                             // --> 20140401-APP20-CMC02.02.00-1939-FEC02
                             {BEFORE
                             IF (L_SalesLine."Price Anomaly Type" <> L_SalesLine."Price Anomaly Type"::" ") AND
                                (L_SalesLine."Anomaly Approved" = FALSE) THEN
                               CurrReport.SKIP;
                             AFTER}
                             IF (L_SalesLine."Price Anomaly Type" = L_SalesLine."Price Anomaly Type"::"No Price") OR
                                (NOT ApprovalsMgt_G.CheckApprSalesLineToInvoice(L_SalesLine)) THEN
                               CurrReport.SKIP;
                             // --< 20140401-APP20-CMC02.02.00-1939-FEC02
                           END ELSE
                           // --> 20130731-App20-CMC02.02.00-FEC01
                           {BEFORE
                             ERROR(Text8053025,FORMAT("Order No."),FORMAT("Order Line No."));
                           AFTER}
                             CurrReport.SKIP;
                           // <-- 20130731-App20-CMC02.02.00-FEC01
                           // <-- 20130604-App20-CMC02.02.00-FDA01

                           IF Type =Type::Item
                           THEN BEGIN
                             Item_G."No." := "No.";
                             IF NOT Item_G.FIND('=') THEN CurrReport.SKIP;
                           END;

                           IF Type =Type::"G/L Account"
                           THEN BEGIN
                             IF "CVO Item Attached Line"<>0
                             THEN BEGIN
                               IF SalesShLine_L.GET("Sales Shipment Line"."Document No.","CVO Item Attached Line")
                               THEN
                                 IF (SalesShLine_L.Type=SalesShLine_L.Type::Item) AND (SalesShLine_L."No."<>'')
                                 THEN BEGIN
                                   Item_G."No." := SalesShLine_L."No.";
                                   IF NOT Item_G.FIND('=') THEN CurrReport.SKIP;
                                 END;
                             END;
                             IF "CVO Corn Item Attached Line"<>0
                             THEN BEGIN
                               IF SalesShLine_L.GET("Sales Shipment Line"."Document No.","CVO Corn Item Attached Line")
                               THEN
                                 IF (SalesShLine_L.Type=SalesShLine_L.Type::Item) AND (SalesShLine_L."No."<>'')
                                 THEN BEGIN
                                   Item_G."No." := SalesShLine_L."No.";
                                   IF NOT Item_G.FIND('=') THEN CurrReport.SKIP;
                                 END;
                             END;
                             IF "RPD Item Attached Line" <>0 THEN BEGIN
                               IF SalesShLine_L.GET("Sales Shipment Line"."Document No.","RPD Item Attached Line") THEN
                                 IF (SalesShLine_L.Type=SalesShLine_L.Type::Item) AND (SalesShLine_L."No."<>'') THEN BEGIN
                                   Item_G."No." := SalesShLine_L."No.";
                                   IF NOT Item_G.FIND('=') THEN CurrReport.SKIP;
                                 END;
                             END;
                           END;



                           // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
                           // G'n'ration des Factures avec Regroupement
                           // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
                           IF G_CreateInvoice THEN BEGIN
                             IF ("Qty. Shipped Not Invoiced" <> 0) OR ("Sales Shipment Line".Type = 0) THEN BEGIN
                               IF ("Bill-to Customer No." <> G_recCustomer."No.") AND ("Sales Shipment Line"."Sell-to Customer No." <> '') THEN
                                 G_recCustomer.GET("Bill-to Customer No.");

                               IF NOT (G_recCustomer.Blocked IN [G_recCustomer.Blocked::All,G_recCustomer.Blocked::Invoice]) THEN BEGIN

                                 //-->20130603-App22-CMC02.02.00-AST01
                                 //BEFORE
                                 {IF (NOT isDocumentHeaderExist(SalesOrderHeader."Document Type"::Invoice,
                                                                SalesOrderHeader."Bill-to Customer No.",
                                                                SalesOrderHeader."Currency Code",
                                                                L_ActivityCode,
                                                                L_InvoiceDueDate))
                                                                OR (CreateSalesHeader_G)
                                                                THEN BEGIN}
                                 //AFTER
                                 IF (NOT isDocumentHeaderExist(SalesOrderHeader."Document Type"::Invoice,
                                                                SalesOrderHeader."Bill-to Customer No.",
                                                                SalesOrderHeader."Currency Code",
                                                                L_ActivityCode,
                                                                L_InvoiceDueDate,
                                                                SalesOrderHeader."Customer Posting Group",
                                                                SalesOrderHeader."Payment Method Code",
                                                                SalesOrderHeader."Group Account Code",PaymTermsCode_L))
                                                                OR (CreateSalesHeader_G)
                                                                THEN BEGIN
                                 //<--20130603-App22-CMC02.02.00-AST01

                                   // 20140414-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-1885-OAR01 -->
                                   // BEFORE
                                   // InsertSalesInvHeader(G_recSalesLine."Document Type"::Invoice, L_ActivityCode, L_InvoiceDueDate,
                                   //    "Sales Shipment Header"."Bill-to Customer No.");
                                   // AFTER

                                   // --> 20140615-APP22-CMC02.02.00-2243-FEC04
                                   {BEFORE
                                   InsertSalesInvHeader(G_recSalesLine."Document Type"::Invoice, L_ActivityCode, L_InvoiceDueDate,
                                                        "Sales Shipment Header"."Bill-to Customer No.",
                                                        GetSalesLinePaymentTerms(1,"Order No.","Order Line No."));
                                   AFTER}
                                   InsertSalesInvHeader(G_recSalesLine."Document Type"::Invoice, L_ActivityCode, L_InvoiceDueDate,
                                                        "Sales Shipment Header"."Bill-to Customer No.",
                                                        PaymTermsCode_L);
                                   // --< 20140615-APP22-CMC02.02.00-2243-FEC04


                                   // <-- 20140414-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-1885-OAR01

                                   // --> 20130906-App29-CMC02.02.00-WSA01
                                   TempSalesInvHeader_G.INIT;
                                   TempSalesInvHeader_G.SetSystemCreatedPayment(TRUE);
                                   TempSalesInvHeader_G.TRANSFERFIELDS(G_recSalesHeader);
                                   TempSalesInvHeader_G.INSERT;
                                   // <-- 20130906-App29-CMC02.02.00-WSA01

                                   G_recSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",G_recSalesHeader."Document Type");
                                   G_recSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",G_recSalesHeader."No.");
                                   G_recSalesLine."Document Type" := G_recSalesHeader."Document Type";
                                   G_recSalesLine."Document No." := G_recSalesHeader."No.";
                                   G_recSalesLine."Shipment No." := '';
                                 END;

                                 G_recSalesShptLine := "Sales Shipment Line";
                                 G_recSalesShptLine.InsertInvLineFromShptLine(G_recSalesLine,G_recTempFromLineDim);

                                 // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
                                 // CAS Ligne Avoir CVO : Mise . jour du no. de ligne article li'
                                 // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
                                 IF G_recSalesLine."CVO Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                                   G_recSalesLine."CVO Item Attached Line" := 0; // Initialisation
                                   L_recSalesShipmentLine.RESET;
                                   IF L_recSalesShipmentLine.GET(G_recSalesLine."Shipment No.", G_recSalesLine."Shipment Line No.") THEN BEGIN
                                     IF L_recSalesShipmentLine.GET(L_recSalesShipmentLine."Document No.",
                                             L_recSalesShipmentLine."CVO Item Attached Line") THEN BEGIN
                                       L_SalesLine := G_recSalesLine;
                                       L_SalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type", G_recSalesLine."Document Type");
                                       L_SalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.", G_recSalesLine."Document No.");
                                       L_SalesLine.SETRANGE("Shipment No.", L_recSalesShipmentLine."Document No.");
                                       L_SalesLine.SETRANGE("Shipment Line No.", L_recSalesShipmentLine."Line No.");
                                       IF L_SalesLine.FINDFIRST THEN
                                         G_recSalesLine."CVO Item Attached Line" := L_SalesLine."Line No.";
                                     END;
                                   END;
                                   G_recSalesLine.MODIFY;
                                 END;

                                 // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
                                 // CAS Ligne Avoir CVO Ma<s : Mise . jour du no. de ligne article li'
                                 // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
                                 IF G_recSalesLine."CVO Corn Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                                   G_recSalesLine."CVO Corn Item Attached Line" := 0; // Initialisation
                                   L_recSalesShipmentLine.RESET;
                                   IF L_recSalesShipmentLine.GET(G_recSalesLine."Shipment No.", G_recSalesLine."Shipment Line No.") THEN BEGIN
                                     IF L_recSalesShipmentLine.GET(L_recSalesShipmentLine."Document No.",
                                       L_recSalesShipmentLine."CVO Corn Item Attached Line") THEN
                                     BEGIN
                                       L_SalesLine := G_recSalesLine;
                                       L_SalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type", G_recSalesLine."Document Type");
                                       L_SalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.", G_recSalesLine."Document No.");
                                       L_SalesLine.SETRANGE("Shipment No.", L_recSalesShipmentLine."Document No.");
                                       L_SalesLine.SETRANGE("Shipment Line No.", L_recSalesShipmentLine."Line No.");
                                       IF L_SalesLine.FINDFIRST THEN
                                         G_recSalesLine."CVO Corn Item Attached Line" := L_SalesLine."Line No.";
                                     END;
                                   END;
                                   G_recSalesLine.MODIFY;
                                 END;

                                 G_cduDimMgt.MoveTempFromDimToTempToDim(G_recTempFromLineDim,G_recTempToLineDim);

                               END ELSE
                                 G_NoOfSalesInvErrors := G_NoOfSalesInvErrors + 1;
                             END;
                           END;

                           G_recSalesLineBis.INIT;
                           IF G_recSalesLineBis.GET(G_recSalesLineBis."Document Type"::Order,"Order No.","Order Line No.") THEN;

                           G_SubTotalAmount += "Sales Shipment Line"."Qty. Shipped Not Invoiced" * G_recSalesLineBis."Unit Price";
                           G_TotalAmount += "Sales Shipment Line"."Qty. Shipped Not Invoiced" * G_recSalesLineBis."Unit Price";
                           G_recSalesLineBis."Unit Price":=ROUND(G_recSalesLineBis."Unit Price",0.001,'>');

                           IF ISSERVICETIER THEN
                             SalesShipmentLineTypeNo := Type;
                         END;

        OnPostDataItem=VAR
                         SalesGetShpt@8053000 : Codeunit 64;
                       BEGIN
                         SalesGetShpt.GetItemChargeAssgnt("Sales Shipment Line");
                       END;

        GroupTotalFields=Document No.;
        DataItemLink=Document No.=FIELD(No.);
      }

                                       ReleaseSalesDoc_L.Reopen(SalesHeader_L);

                                 END;

                             END;
                           END;

                             IF SalesHeader_L.Status <> SalesHeader_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
                               CLEAR(ReleaseSalesDoc_L);

                             END;
                           END;

                         END;

                         //--> 20140324-APP22-CMC02.02.00-1896-FHS01
                         EndReport();

    LOCAL PROCEDURE isDocumentHeaderExist@8053000(P_DocumentType@8053000 : 'Quote,Order,Invoice,Credit Memo,Blanket Order,Return Order';P_CustomerNo@8053001 : Code[20];P_CurrencyCode@8053002 : Code[10];P_ActivityCode@8053003 : Code[10];P_DueDate@8053004 : Date;CustPostingGr_P@8053006 : Code[10];PaymentMethCode_P@8053007 : Code[10];GroupAccCode_P@1000000000 : Code[10];PaymTermsCode_P@1000000001 : Code[10]) Res_HeaderExists : Boolean;
    VAR
      L_recSalesHeader@8053005 : Record 36;
    BEGIN
      // -------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // D'termine si une ent^te de regroupement de BL a d'j. 't' cr''
      // -------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Res_HeaderExists := FALSE;

      L_recSalesHeader := G_recSalesHeader;

      L_recSalesHeader.RESET;
      L_recSalesHeader.SETCURRENTKEY("Document Type", "Bill-to Customer No.");
      L_recSalesHeader.SETRANGE("Document Type", P_DocumentType);
      L_recSalesHeader.SETRANGE("Bill-to Customer No.", P_CustomerNo);
      L_recSalesHeader.SETRANGE("Currency Code", P_CurrencyCode);
      //-->20130603-App22-CMC02.02.00-AST01
      L_recSalesHeader.SETRANGE("Customer Posting Group", CustPostingGr_P);
      L_recSalesHeader.SETRANGE("Payment Method Code", PaymentMethCode_P);
      //<--20130603-App22-CMC02.02.00-AST01

      // --> 20140612-APP22-CMC02.02.00-2225-FEC01
      L_recSalesHeader.SETRANGE("Group Account Code",GroupAccCode_P);
      // --< 20140612-APP22-CMC02.02.00-2225-FEC01

      //--> 20132810-App22-CMC02.02.00-1010-FHU01
      // --> 20130627-App22-CMC02.02.00-MMI01
      //BEFORE
      //IF G_AllActivity = FALSE THEN
      //AFTER
      // <-- 20130627-App22-CMC02.02.00-MMI01
      IF G_AllActivity = FALSE THEN
      //<-- 20132810-App22-CMC02.02.00-1010-FHU01
        L_recSalesHeader.SETRANGE("Activity Code", P_ActivityCode);

      // --> 20140615-APP22-CMC02.02.00-2243-FEC01
      {BEFORE
      L_recSalesHeader.SETRANGE("Due Date", P_DueDate);
      AFTER}
      IF P_DueDate <> 0D THEN
        L_recSalesHeader.SETRANGE("Due Date", P_DueDate);
      IF PaymTermsCode_P <> '' THEN
        L_recSalesHeader.SETRANGE("Payment Terms Code", PaymTermsCode_P);
      // --< 20140615-APP22-CMC02.02.00-2243-FEC01

      L_recSalesHeader.SETRANGE("External Document No.", '#TEMPORARY#');
      IF L_recSalesHeader.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          G_recSalesHeader.GET(L_recSalesHeader."Document Type", L_recSalesHeader."No.");
          IF VerifyDimAnalogy THEN BEGIN
            Res_HeaderExists := TRUE;
            G_recSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",G_recSalesHeader."Document Type");
            G_recSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",G_recSalesHeader."No.");
            G_recSalesLine."Document Type" := G_recSalesHeader."Document Type";
            G_recSalesLine."Document No." := G_recSalesHeader."No.";
          END;
        UNTIL L_recSalesHeader.NEXT = 0;
      END;

      EXIT(Res_HeaderExists);
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE FinalizeSalesInvHeader@2();
    BEGIN
      WITH G_recSalesHeader DO BEGIN
        IF G_CalcInvDisc THEN BEGIN
          G_recSalesLine.RESET;
          G_recSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",G_recSalesHeader."Document Type");
          G_recSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",G_recSalesHeader."No.");
          IF G_recSalesLine.FINDSET THEN
          G_cduSalesCalcDisc.RUN(G_recSalesLine);
        END;

        G_recSalesHeader.FIND;
        G_recSalesHeader."External Document No." := '';
        G_recSalesHeader."Batch Agro Lot No." := G_recSalesHeader."Posting Date";
        G_recSalesHeader.MODIFY;

        G_cduDimMgt.TransferTempToDimToDocDim(G_recTempToLineDim);
        COMMIT;

        CLEAR(G_cduSalesCalcDisc);
        CLEAR(G_cduSalesPost);
        G_NoOfSalesInv := G_NoOfSalesInv + 1;

        IF G_PostInv THEN BEGIN
          CLEAR(G_cduSalesPost);
          IF NOT G_cduSalesPost.RUN(G_recSalesHeader) THEN
            G_NoOfSalesInvErrors := G_NoOfSalesInvErrors + 1;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE FinalizeSalesCrMHeader@8053005();
    BEGIN
      WITH G_recSalesHeader DO BEGIN

        IF G_CalcCrMemoDisc THEN BEGIN
          G_recSalesLine.RESET;
          G_recSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",G_recSalesHeader."Document Type");
          G_recSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",G_recSalesHeader."No.");
          IF G_recSalesLine.FINDSET THEN
          G_cduSalesCalcDisc.RUN(G_recSalesLine);
        END;

        G_recSalesHeader.FIND;
        G_recSalesHeader."External Document No." := '';
        G_recSalesHeader."Batch Agro Lot No." := G_recSalesHeader."Posting Date";
        G_recSalesHeader.MODIFY;

        G_cduDimMgt.TransferTempToDimToDocDim(G_recTempToLineDim);
        COMMIT;

        CLEAR(G_cduSalesCalcDisc);
        CLEAR(G_cduSalesPost);
        G_NoOfSalesCrM := G_NoOfSalesCrM + 1;

        IF G_PostCreditMemo THEN BEGIN
          CLEAR(G_cduSalesPost);
          IF NOT G_cduSalesPost.RUN(G_recSalesHeader) THEN
            G_NoOfSalesCrMErrors := G_NoOfSalesCrMErrors + 1;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE InsertSalesInvHeader@1(P_DocumentType@8053000 : 'Quote,Order,Invoice,Credit Memo,Blanket Order,Return Order';P_ActivityCode@8053001 : Code[10];P_DueDate@8053002 : Date;P_BillToCust@8053003 : Code[20];P_PaymentTerms@8053009 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      L_CustActivity@8053004 : Record 8053019;
      FarmerRec_L@8053005 : Record 5050;
      ContactPayMethodRec_L@8053006 : Record 8053255;
      PaymentmethodRec_L@8053007 : Record 289;
      CMCSetup_L@8053008 : Record 8052982;
    BEGIN
      WITH G_recSalesHeader DO BEGIN
        INIT;
        "Document Type" := P_DocumentType;
        "No." := '';
      // --> 20130906-App29-CMC02.02.00-WSA01
        G_recSalesHeader.SetSystemCreatedPayment(TRUE);
      // <-- 20130906-App29-CMC02.02.00-WSA01
        //--> 20132810-App22-CMC02.02.00-1010-FHU01
        G_recSalesHeader.SetHideCollectifChoice(TRUE);
        //<-- 20132810-App22-CMC02.02.00-1010-FHU01
        INSERT(TRUE);
        VALIDATE("Sell-to Customer No.",P_BillToCust);
        // --> 20140414-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-1885-OAR01
        IF P_PaymentTerms <> '' THEN
          G_recSalesHeader.VALIDATE("Payment Terms Code",P_PaymentTerms);
        // <-- 20140414-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-1885-OAR01
        VALIDATE("Posting Date", G_PostingDateReq);
        VALIDATE("Document Date",G_DocumentDateReq);
        VALIDATE("Currency Code",SalesOrderHeader."Currency Code");
        //--> 20140314-APP22-CMC02.02.00-1832-FHS01
        VALIDATE("Fixed Due Date", FixedDueDate_G);
        //<-- 20140314-APP22-CMC02.02.00-1832-FHS01
        //-->20130603-App22-CMC02.02.00-AST01
        IF "Document Type" = "Document Type"::Invoice THEN BEGIN
          VALIDATE("Customer Posting Group",SalesOrderHeader."Customer Posting Group");
          VALIDATE("Payment Method Code", SalesOrderHeader."Payment Method Code");
          // --> 20140612-APP22-CMC02.02.00-2225-FEC02
          "Group Account Code" := SalesOrderHeader."Group Account Code";
          // --< 20140612-APP22-CMC02.02.00-2225-FEC02
        END;
        IF "Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Credit Memo" THEN BEGIN
          VALIDATE("Customer Posting Group",SalesReturnOrderHeader."Customer Posting Group");
          VALIDATE("Payment Method Code", SalesReturnOrderHeader."Payment Method Code");
          // --> 20140612-APP22-CMC02.02.00-2225-FEC03
          "Group Account Code" := SalesReturnOrderHeader."Group Account Code";
          // --< 20140612-APP22-CMC02.02.00-2225-FEC03
        END;
        //<--20130603-App22-CMC02.02.00-AST01
        "Shortcut Dimension 1 Code" := SalesOrderHeader."Shortcut Dimension 1 Code";
        "Shortcut Dimension 2 Code" := SalesOrderHeader."Shortcut Dimension 2 Code";
        "External Document No." := '#TEMPORARY#';
        //--> 20132810-App22-CMC02.02.00-1010-FHU01
        // --> 20130627-App22-CMC02.02.00-MMI01
        //BEFORE
        //IF G_AllActivity = FALSE THEN
        //AFTER
        // <-- 20130627-App22-CMC02.02.00-MMI01
        IF G_AllActivity = FALSE THEN
        //<-- 20132810-App22-CMC02.02.00-1010-FHU01
          "Activity Code" := P_ActivityCode;
        // --> 20140615-APP22-CMC02.02.00-2243-FEC02
        {BEFORE
        "Due Date" := P_DueDate;
        AFTER}
        IF P_DueDate <> 0D THEN
          "Due Date" := P_DueDate;
        // --< 20140615-APP22-CMC02.02.00-2243-FEC02
        IF G_AllActivity = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          IF L_CustActivity.GET("Bill-to Customer No.",P_ActivityCode) THEN
            IF L_CustActivity."Payment Method Code"<>'' THEN
              G_recSalesHeader.VALIDATE("Payment Method Code",L_CustActivity."Payment Method Code");
        END;
        "Prescription No." := SalesOrderHeader."Prescription No.";
        G_recSalesHeader."Vetenarian Contact Code" := SalesOrderHeader."Vetenarian Contact Code";
        G_recSalesHeader."Vetenarian Name" := SalesOrderHeader."Vetenarian Name";
        G_recSalesHeader."Vetenarian Adress 1" := SalesOrderHeader."Vetenarian Adress 1";
        G_recSalesHeader."Vetenarian Adress 2" := SalesOrderHeader."Vetenarian Adress 2";
        G_recSalesHeader."Vetenarian Post Code" := SalesOrderHeader."Vetenarian Post Code";
        G_recSalesHeader."Vetenarian City" := SalesOrderHeader."Vetenarian City";
        G_recSalesHeader."Vetenarian County" := SalesOrderHeader."Vetenarian County";
        G_recSalesHeader."Vetenarian Country Code" := SalesOrderHeader."Vetenarian Country Code";
        //-->20130603-App22-CMC02.02.00-AST01
        VALIDATE(G_recSalesHeader."Responsibility Center", RespCenter_G);
        VALIDATE(G_recSalesHeader."Assigned User ID", CurrentUserID_G);
        //<--20130603-App22-CMC02.02.00-AST01
        //--> 20131129-App22-CMC02.02.00-1183-FHU01
        "Prod. Activity Code" := ProdActCode_G;
        //<-- 20131129-App22-CMC02.02.00-1183-FHU01

        // --> 20131217-Gen19/001-CMC02.02.00-FDA01
        "Grouping Code" := GroupingCode_G;
        // <-- 20131217-Gen19/001-CMC02.02.00-FDA01

        MODIFY;

        G_recToDocDim.RESET;
        G_recToDocDim.SETRANGE("Table ID", DATABASE::"Sales Header");
        G_recToDocDim.SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type");
        G_recToDocDim.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
        G_recToDocDim.SETRANGE("Line No.", 0);
        G_recToDocDim.DELETEALL;

        IF G_recFromDocDim.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            G_recToDocDim.INIT;
            G_recToDocDim."Table ID" := DATABASE::"Sales Header";
            G_recToDocDim."Document Type" := "Document Type";
            G_recToDocDim."Document No." := "No.";
            G_recToDocDim."Line No." := 0;
            G_recToDocDim."Dimension Code" := G_recFromDocDim."Dimension Code";
            G_recToDocDim."Dimension Value Code" := G_recFromDocDim."Dimension Value Code";
            //--> 20140410-Cer42/001-CMC02.02.00-FHS01
            //BEFORE
            //G_recToDocDim.INSERT;
            //AFTER
            //GSre le cas o- plusieurs documents associ's . la facture ont le m^me axe analytique
            IF G_recToDocDim.INSERT THEN ;
            //<-- 20140410-Cer42/001-CMC02.02.00-FHS01
          UNTIL G_recFromDocDim.NEXT = 0;
        END;

        G_NextLineNo := 10000;

      CreateSalesHeader_G:=FALSE;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE VerifyDimAnalogy@3() : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      IF NOT G_recFromDocDim.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
        G_recFromDocDim.INIT;
        G_recFromDocDim."Dimension Code" := '';
      END;

      G_recToDocDim.SETRANGE("Table ID",DATABASE::"Sales Header");
      G_recToDocDim.SETRANGE("Document Type",G_recSalesHeader."Document Type");
      G_recToDocDim.SETRANGE("Document No.",G_recSalesHeader."No.");
      IF NOT G_recToDocDim.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
        G_recToDocDim.INIT;
        G_recToDocDim."Dimension Code" := '';
      END;

      IF G_recFromDocDim.FIND('-') THEN
        REPEAT
          IF (G_recFromDocDim."Dimension Code" <> G_recToDocDim."Dimension Code") OR
             (G_recFromDocDim."Dimension Value Code" <> G_recToDocDim."Dimension Value Code")
          THEN
            EXIT(FALSE);
        UNTIL (G_recFromDocDim.NEXT = 0) AND (G_recToDocDim.NEXT = 0);

      EXIT(TRUE);
    END;

    PROCEDURE InitializeReport@5(P_PostingDate@1002 : Date;P_DocDate@1003 : Date);
    BEGIN
      G_PostingDateReq  := P_PostingDate;
      G_DocumentDateReq := P_DocDate;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetHideDialog@7(P_NewHideDialog@1000 : Boolean);
    BEGIN
      G_HideDialog := P_NewHideDialog;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetActivityCodeForShipment@8053001(P_recSalesShipmentLine@8053000 : Record 111) Res_ActivityCode : Code[10];
    VAR
      L_recSalesShipmentLine@8053002 : Record 111;
      L_recItem@8053001 : Record 27;
      SalesLine_L@8053003 : Record 37;
    BEGIN
      Res_ActivityCode := '';

      L_recSalesShipmentLine := P_recSalesShipmentLine;

      // --> 20140327-App12-CMC02.02.00-1911-OAR01
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne de frais annexe : on prend le code activit' de la ligne de frais annexe
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Sales Shipment Line".Type = "Sales Shipment Line".Type::"Charge (Item)" THEN
        Res_ActivityCode := "Sales Shipment Line"."Activity Code";
      // <-- 20140327-App12-CMC02.02.00-1911-OAR01

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir CVO : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Sales Shipment Line"."CVO Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF L_recSalesShipmentLine.GET(P_recSalesShipmentLine."Document No.", P_recSalesShipmentLine."CVO Item Attached Line") THEN;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir CVO ma<s : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Sales Shipment Line"."CVO Corn Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF L_recSalesShipmentLine.GET(P_recSalesShipmentLine."Document No.",
                        P_recSalesShipmentLine."CVO Corn Item Attached Line") THEN;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir RPD : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Sales Shipment Line"."RPD Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF L_recSalesShipmentLine.GET(P_recSalesShipmentLine."Document No.", P_recSalesShipmentLine."RPD Item Attached Line") THEN;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // R'cup'ration du code activit' de l'article livr'
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF P_recSalesShipmentLine.Type <> P_recSalesShipmentLine.Type::" " THEN BEGIN
        // --> 20131010-App22-CMC02.02.00-0864-MMI01
        //BEFORE
        //IF L_recItem.GET(L_recSalesShipmentLine."No.") THEN
        //  Res_ActivityCode := L_recItem."Activity Code"
        //AFTER
        IF SalesLine_L.GET(SalesLine_L."Document Type"::Order, L_recSalesShipmentLine."Order No.",
          L_recSalesShipmentLine."Order Line No.") THEN
            Res_ActivityCode := SalesLine_L."Activity Code";
        // <-- 20131010-App22-CMC02.02.00-0864-MMI01
      END ELSE BEGIN
        L_recSalesShipmentLine.SETRANGE("Document No.", P_recSalesShipmentLine."Document No.");
        L_recSalesShipmentLine.SETRANGE(Type, L_recSalesShipmentLine.Type::Item);
        L_recSalesShipmentLine.SETFILTER("Line No.", '<%1', P_recSalesShipmentLine."Line No.");
        IF L_recSalesShipmentLine.FINDFIRST THEN
          IF L_recItem.GET(L_recSalesShipmentLine."No.") THEN
            Res_ActivityCode := L_recItem."Activity Code";
      END;

      EXIT(Res_ActivityCode);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetDueDateForShipment@8053004(P_recSalesShipmentLine@8053000 : Record 111;P_PostingDate@8053001 : Date) Res_DueDate : Date;
    VAR
      L_recPaymentTerms@8053002 : Record 3;
      L_recSalesShipmentLine@8053003 : Record 111;
      L_recSalesLine@8053004 : Record 37;
      L_recSalesHeader@8053005 : Record 36;
    BEGIN
      Res_DueDate := 0D;

      L_recSalesShipmentLine := P_recSalesShipmentLine;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir CVO : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Sales Shipment Line"."CVO Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF L_recSalesShipmentLine.GET(P_recSalesShipmentLine."Document No.", P_recSalesShipmentLine."CVO Item Attached Line") THEN;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir CVO : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Sales Shipment Line"."CVO Corn Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF L_recSalesShipmentLine.GET(P_recSalesShipmentLine."Document No.",
            P_recSalesShipmentLine."CVO Corn Item Attached Line") THEN;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir RPD : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Sales Shipment Line"."RPD Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF L_recSalesShipmentLine.GET(P_recSalesShipmentLine."Document No.", P_recSalesShipmentLine."RPD Item Attached Line") THEN;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Calcul de la date d''ch'ance de facturation de l'article livr'
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF P_recSalesShipmentLine.Type = P_recSalesShipmentLine.Type::" " THEN BEGIN
        L_recSalesShipmentLine.SETRANGE("Document No.", P_recSalesShipmentLine."Document No.");
        L_recSalesShipmentLine.SETRANGE(Type, L_recSalesShipmentLine.Type::Item);
        L_recSalesShipmentLine.SETFILTER("Line No.", '<%1', P_recSalesShipmentLine."Line No.");
        IF L_recSalesShipmentLine.FINDFIRST THEN;
      END;

      IF P_recSalesShipmentLine."Qty. Shipped Not Invoiced"<>0 THEN BEGIN
        L_recSalesLine.GET(L_recSalesLine."Document Type"::Order,L_recSalesShipmentLine."Order No.",
            L_recSalesShipmentLine."Order Line No.");

        IF L_recPaymentTerms.GET(L_recSalesLine."Payment Terms Code") THEN
          Res_DueDate := CALCDATE(L_recPaymentTerms."Due Date Calculation", G_PostingDateReq);

        IF L_recSalesLine."Due Date" <> 0D THEN
          Res_DueDate := L_recSalesLine."Due Date";

        IF (L_recSalesLine."Due Date" = 0D) AND (L_recSalesLine."Payment Terms Code"='')
        THEN BEGIN
          L_recSalesHeader.GET(L_recSalesLine."Document Type",L_recSalesLine."Document No.");
          IF L_recPaymentTerms.GET( L_recSalesHeader."Payment Terms Code") THEN
           Res_DueDate := CALCDATE(L_recPaymentTerms."Due Date Calculation", G_PostingDateReq);
        END;
      END;

      EXIT(Res_DueDate);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetActivityCodeForReturn@8053009(P_recReturnReceiptLine@8053000 : Record 6661) Res_ActivityCode : Code[10];
    VAR
      L_recItem@8053001 : Record 27;
      L_recReturnReceiptLine@8053002 : Record 6661;
      SalesLine_L@8053003 : Record 37;
    BEGIN
      Res_ActivityCode := '';

       L_recReturnReceiptLine := P_recReturnReceiptLine;
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir CVO : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Return Receipt Line"."CVO Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF L_recReturnReceiptLine.GET(P_recReturnReceiptLine."Document No.", P_recReturnReceiptLine."CVO Item Attached Line") THEN;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir CVO : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Return Receipt Line"."CVO Corn Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF L_recReturnReceiptLine.GET(P_recReturnReceiptLine."Document No.",
              P_recReturnReceiptLine."CVO Corn Item Attached Line") THEN;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir RPD : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Return Receipt Line"."RPD Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF L_recReturnReceiptLine.GET(P_recReturnReceiptLine."Document No.", P_recReturnReceiptLine."RPD Item Attached Line") THEN;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // R'cup'ration du code activit' de l'article livr'
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF P_recReturnReceiptLine.Type <> P_recReturnReceiptLine.Type::" " THEN BEGIN
        // --> 20131010-App22-CMC02.02.00-0864-MMI01
        //BEFORE
        //IF L_recItem.GET(L_recReturnReceiptLine."No.") THEN
        //  Res_ActivityCode := L_recItem."Activity Code"
        //AFTER
        IF SalesLine_L.GET(SalesLine_L."Document Type"::"Return Order", L_recReturnReceiptLine."Return Order No.",
          L_recReturnReceiptLine."Return Order Line No.") THEN
            Res_ActivityCode := SalesLine_L."Activity Code";
        // <-- 20131010-App22-CMC02.02.00-0864-MMI01
      END ELSE BEGIN
        L_recReturnReceiptLine.SETRANGE("Document No.", P_recReturnReceiptLine."Document No.");
        L_recReturnReceiptLine.SETRANGE(Type, L_recReturnReceiptLine.Type::Item);
        L_recReturnReceiptLine.SETFILTER("Line No.", '<%1', P_recReturnReceiptLine."Line No.");
        IF L_recReturnReceiptLine.FINDFIRST THEN
          IF L_recItem.GET(L_recReturnReceiptLine."No.") THEN
            Res_ActivityCode := L_recItem."Activity Code";
      END;

      EXIT(Res_ActivityCode);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetDueDateForReturn@8053008(P_recReturnReceiptLine@8053000 : Record 6661;P_PostingDate@8053001 : Date) Res_DueDate : Date;
    VAR
      L_recReturnReceiptHeader@8053002 : Record 6660;
      L_recReturnReceiptLine@8053003 : Record 6661;
      L_recPaymentTerms@8053004 : Record 3;
      L_recSalesLine@8053005 : Record 37;
      L_recSalesHeader@8053006 : Record 36;
    BEGIN
      Res_DueDate := 0D;

      L_recReturnReceiptLine := P_recReturnReceiptLine;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir CVO : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Return Receipt Line"."CVO Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF L_recReturnReceiptLine.GET(P_recReturnReceiptLine."Document No.", P_recReturnReceiptLine."CVO Item Attached Line") THEN;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir CVO : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Return Receipt Line"."CVO Corn Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF L_recReturnReceiptLine.GET(P_recReturnReceiptLine."Document No.",
                        P_recReturnReceiptLine."CVO Corn Item Attached Line") THEN;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir RPD : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Return Receipt Line"."RPD Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF L_recReturnReceiptLine.GET(P_recReturnReceiptLine."Document No.", P_recReturnReceiptLine."RPD Item Attached Line") THEN;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Calcul de la date d''ch'ance de facturation de l'article livr'
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF P_recReturnReceiptLine.Type = P_recReturnReceiptLine.Type::" " THEN BEGIN
        L_recReturnReceiptLine.SETRANGE("Document No.", P_recReturnReceiptLine."Document No.");
        L_recReturnReceiptLine.SETRANGE(Type, L_recReturnReceiptLine.Type::Item);
        L_recReturnReceiptLine.SETFILTER("Line No.", '<%1', P_recReturnReceiptLine."Line No.");
        IF L_recReturnReceiptLine.FINDFIRST THEN;
      END;

      IF P_recReturnReceiptLine."Return Qty. Rcd. Not Invd."<>0 THEN BEGIN
        L_recSalesLine.GET(L_recSalesLine."Document Type"::"Return Order",L_recReturnReceiptLine."Return Order No.",
            L_recReturnReceiptLine."Return Order Line No.");

        IF L_recPaymentTerms.GET(L_recSalesLine."Payment Terms Code") THEN
          Res_DueDate := CALCDATE(L_recPaymentTerms."Due Date Calculation", G_PostingDateReq);

        IF L_recSalesLine."Due Date" <> 0D THEN
          Res_DueDate := L_recSalesLine."Due Date";

        IF (L_recSalesLine."Due Date" = 0D) AND (L_recSalesLine."Payment Terms Code"='')
        THEN BEGIN
          L_recSalesHeader.GET(L_recSalesLine."Document Type",L_recSalesLine."Document No.");
          IF L_recPaymentTerms.GET( L_recSalesHeader."Payment Terms Code") THEN
           Res_DueDate := CALCDATE(L_recPaymentTerms."Due Date Calculation", G_PostingDateReq);
        END;

      END;

      EXIT(Res_DueDate);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetSalesPersonCodeForShipment@8053003(recSalesShipmentLine_P@8053000 : Record 111) SalesPersonCode_Res : Code[20];
    VAR
      recSalesShipmentLine_L@8053001 : Record 111;
      recSalesLine_L@8053002 : Record 37;
    BEGIN
      //-->20130603-App22-CMC02.02.00-AST01
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Gets the salesperson code from the shipment
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Method parameters:
      //  recSalesShipmentLine_P - a sales shipment line record
      //
      // Return Parameters:
      //  SalesPersonCode_Res - the salesperson's code
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      SalesPersonCode_Res := '';
      recSalesLine_L.RESET;
      recSalesLine_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Document Type","Document No.","Line No.");

      recSalesShipmentLine_L := recSalesShipmentLine_P;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir CVO : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Sales Shipment Line"."CVO Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF recSalesShipmentLine_L.GET(recSalesShipmentLine_P."Document No.", recSalesShipmentLine_P."CVO Item Attached Line") THEN;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir CVO ma<s : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Sales Shipment Line"."CVO Corn Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF recSalesShipmentLine_L.GET(recSalesShipmentLine_P."Document No.",
                        recSalesShipmentLine_P."CVO Corn Item Attached Line") THEN;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir RPD : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Sales Shipment Line"."RPD Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF recSalesShipmentLine_L.GET(recSalesShipmentLine_P."Document No.", recSalesShipmentLine_P."RPD Item Attached Line") THEN;

      IF recSalesShipmentLine_P.Type <> recSalesShipmentLine_P.Type::" " THEN BEGIN

        IF recSalesLine_L.GET(recSalesLine_L."Document Type"::Order
                             , recSalesShipmentLine_L."Order No."
                             , recSalesShipmentLine_L."Order Line No.") THEN
            SalesPersonCode_Res := recSalesLine_L."Salesperson Code";

      END
      ELSE BEGIN
        recSalesShipmentLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", recSalesShipmentLine_P."Document No.");
        recSalesShipmentLine_P.SETRANGE(Type, recSalesShipmentLine_L.Type::Item);
        recSalesShipmentLine_L.SETFILTER("Line No.", '<%1', recSalesShipmentLine_P."Line No.");

        IF recSalesLine_L.GET(recSalesLine_L."Document Type"::Order
                             , recSalesShipmentLine_L."Order No."
                             , recSalesShipmentLine_L."Order Line No.") THEN
            SalesPersonCode_Res := recSalesLine_L."Salesperson Code";
      END;

      EXIT(SalesPersonCode_Res);
      //<--20130603-App22-CMC02.02.00-AST01
    END;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Method parameters:
      //  recSalesShipmentLine_P - a sales shipment line record
      //
      // Return Parameters:
      //  CampNo_Res - sales network code
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      CampNo_Res := '';
      //--> 20131129-App22-CMC02.02.00-538-FHU01
      CountryVar_P := '';
      //<-- 20131129-App22-CMC02.02.00-538-FHU01
      recSalesLine_L.RESET;
      recSalesLine_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Document Type","Document No.","Line No.");

      recSalesShipmentLine_L := recSalesShipmentLine_P;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir CVO : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Sales Shipment Line"."CVO Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF recSalesShipmentLine_L.GET(recSalesShipmentLine_P."Document No.", recSalesShipmentLine_P."CVO Item Attached Line") THEN;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir CVO ma<s : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Sales Shipment Line"."CVO Corn Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF recSalesShipmentLine_L.GET(recSalesShipmentLine_P."Document No.",
                        recSalesShipmentLine_P."CVO Corn Item Attached Line") THEN;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir RPD : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Sales Shipment Line"."RPD Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF recSalesShipmentLine_L.GET(recSalesShipmentLine_P."Document No.", recSalesShipmentLine_P."RPD Item Attached Line") THEN;

      IF recSalesShipmentLine_P.Type <> recSalesShipmentLine_P.Type::" " THEN BEGIN

        IF recSalesLine_L.GET(recSalesLine_L."Document Type"::Order
                             , recSalesShipmentLine_L."Order No."
                             , recSalesShipmentLine_L."Order Line No.") THEN BEGIN
          CampNo_Res := recSalesLine_L."CMC Campaign No.";
        //--> 20131129-App22-CMC02.02.00-538-FHU01
          CountryVar_P := recSalesLine_L."Sales Area Code";
        END;
        //<-- 20131129-App22-CMC02.02.00-538-FHU01

      END
      ELSE BEGIN
        recSalesShipmentLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", recSalesShipmentLine_P."Document No.");
        recSalesShipmentLine_P.SETRANGE(Type, recSalesShipmentLine_L.Type::Item);
        recSalesShipmentLine_L.SETFILTER("Line No.", '<%1', recSalesShipmentLine_P."Line No.");

        IF recSalesLine_L.GET(recSalesLine_L."Document Type"::Order
                             , recSalesShipmentLine_L."Order No."
                             , recSalesShipmentLine_L."Order Line No.") THEN BEGIN
          CampNo_Res := recSalesLine_L."CMC Campaign No.";
        //--> 20131129-App22-CMC02.02.00-538-FHU01
          CountryVar_P := recSalesLine_L."Sales Area Code";
        END;
        //<-- 20131129-App22-CMC02.02.00-538-FHU01
      END;

      EXIT(CampNo_Res);
      //<--20130701-App22-CMC02.02.00-AST01
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCampNoAndCountyForReturn@8053015(recReturnReceiptLine_P@8053000 : Record 6661;VAR CountryVar_P@8053003 : Code[10]) CampNo_Res : Code[10];
    VAR
      recReturnReceiptLine_L@8053001 : Record 6661;
      recSalesLine_L@8053002 : Record 37;
    BEGIN
      //-->20130701-App22-CMC02.02.00-AST01
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Gets the Campaign no. from the return receipt
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Method parameters:
      //  recReturnReceiptLine_P - a return receipt line record
      //
      // Return Parameters:
      //  CampNo_Res - network code
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      CampNo_Res := '';
      //--> 20131129-App22-CMC02.02.00-538-FHU01
      CountryVar_P := '';
      //<-- 20131129-App22-CMC02.02.00-538-FHU01
      recSalesLine_L.RESET;
      recSalesLine_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Document Type","Document No.","Line No.");

      recReturnReceiptLine_L := recReturnReceiptLine_P;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir CVO : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Return Receipt Line"."CVO Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF recReturnReceiptLine_L.GET(recReturnReceiptLine_P."Document No.", recReturnReceiptLine_P."CVO Item Attached Line") THEN;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir CVO ma<s : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Return Receipt Line"."CVO Corn Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF recReturnReceiptLine_L.GET(recReturnReceiptLine_P."Document No.",
                        recReturnReceiptLine_P."CVO Corn Item Attached Line") THEN;

      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CAS : Ligne Avoir RPD : R'cup'ration de la ligne Article associ'e
      // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF "Return Receipt Line"."RPD Item Attached Line" <> 0 THEN
        IF recReturnReceiptLine_L.GET(recReturnReceiptLine_P."Document No.", recReturnReceiptLine_P."RPD Item Attached Line") THEN;


      IF recReturnReceiptLine_P.Type <> recReturnReceiptLine_P.Type::" " THEN BEGIN

        IF recSalesLine_L.GET(recSalesLine_L."Document Type"::"Return Order"
                             , recReturnReceiptLine_L."Return Order No."
                             , recReturnReceiptLine_L."Return Order Line No.") THEN BEGIN
          CampNo_Res := recSalesLine_L."CMC Campaign No.";
          //--> 20131129-App22-CMC02.02.00-538-FHU01
          CountryVar_P := recSalesLine_L."Sales Area Code";
          //<-- 20131129-App22-CMC02.02.00-538-FHU01
        END
      END
      ELSE BEGIN
        recReturnReceiptLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", recReturnReceiptLine_P."Document No.");
        recReturnReceiptLine_P.SETRANGE(Type, recReturnReceiptLine_L.Type::Item);
        recReturnReceiptLine_L.SETFILTER("Line No.", '<%1', recReturnReceiptLine_P."Line No.");

        IF recSalesLine_L.GET(recSalesLine_L."Document Type"::"Return Order"
                             , recReturnReceiptLine_L."Return Order No."
                             , recReturnReceiptLine_L."Return Order Line No.") THEN BEGIN
          CampNo_Res := recSalesLine_L."CMC Campaign No.";
          //--> 20131129-App22-CMC02.02.00-538-FHU01
          CountryVar_P := recSalesLine_L."Sales Area Code";
        END;
        //<-- 20131129-App22-CMC02.02.00-538-FHU01

      END;

      EXIT(CampNo_Res);
      //<--20130701-App22-CMC02.02.00-AST01
    END;

    PROCEDURE CalcAmount@8053017(Cust_P@8053000 : Code[20];DocumentType_P@8053001 : 'Invoice,Credit Memo') : Decimal;
    VAR
      SalesHeader_L@8053002 : Record 36;
      TotalSalesLine_L@8053009 : Record 37;
      TotalSalesLineLCY_L@8053008 : Record 37;
      Cust_L@8053007 : Record 18;
      TotalAmount2_L@8053006 : Decimal;
      SalesLine_L@8053005 : Record 37;
      TempSalesLine_L@8053004 : TEMPORARY Record 37;
      SalesPost_L@8053003 : Codeunit 80;
      TempVATAmountLine1_L@8053010 : TEMPORARY Record 290;
      VATAmount_L@8053011 : Decimal;
      VATAmountText_L@8053012 : Text[30];
      ProfitLCY_L@8053017 : Decimal;
      ProfitPct_L@8053016 : Decimal;
      AdjProfitLCY_L@8053015 : Decimal;
      AdjProfitPct_L@8053014 : Decimal;
      TotalAdjCostLCY_L@8053013 : Decimal;
      Amount_L@8053018 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      //-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //Calucle du Montant TOTAL TTC pour le client en paramStre
      //Entr'es
      // - Cust_P : Record Customer
      // - DocumentType_P : Option type document
      //Sorties
      // - la fonction retourne une valeur decimal
      //-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      // --> 20130906-App29-CMC02.02.00-WSA01
      SalesHeader_L.RESET;
      SalesHeader_L.SETRANGE("Sell-to Customer No.",Cust_P);
      IF DocumentType_P = DocumentType_P::Invoice THEN
         SalesHeader_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",SalesHeader_L."Document Type"::Invoice);
      IF DocumentType_P = DocumentType_P::"Credit Memo" THEN
         SalesHeader_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",SalesHeader_L."Document Type"::"Credit Memo");
      // --> 20140423-APP29-CMC02.02.00-2013-FEC01
      {BEFORE
      SalesHeader_L.SETFILTER("No.",'<>%1',G_recSalesHeader."No.");
      AFTER}
      IF DocumentType_P = DocumentType_P::Invoice THEN
        SalesHeader_L.SETFILTER("No.",'<%1',G_recSalesHeader."No.");
      // --< 20140423-APP29-CMC02.02.00-2013-FEC01
      CLEAR(Amount_L);
      IF SalesHeader_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT

          CLEAR(TotalAmount2_L);
          CLEAR(SalesLine_L);
          CLEAR(TotalSalesLine_L);
          CLEAR(TotalSalesLineLCY_L);
          TempSalesLine_L.DELETEALL;
          CLEAR(TempSalesLine_L);
          CLEAR(SalesPost_L);
          SalesPost_L.GetSalesLines(SalesHeader_L,TempSalesLine_L,0);
          CLEAR(SalesPost_L);
          SalesLine_L.CalcVATAmountLines(0,SalesHeader_L,TempSalesLine_L,TempVATAmountLine1_L);
          SalesPost_L.SumSalesLinesTemp(
           SalesHeader_L,TempSalesLine_L,0,TotalSalesLine_L,TotalSalesLineLCY_L,
                 VATAmount_L,VATAmountText_L,ProfitLCY_L,ProfitPct_L,TotalAdjCostLCY_L);
          TotalAdjCostLCY_L := TotalSalesLineLCY_L."Unit Cost (LCY)";
          AdjProfitLCY_L := TotalSalesLineLCY_L.Amount - TotalAdjCostLCY_L;
          IF TotalSalesLineLCY_L.Amount <> 0 THEN
            AdjProfitPct_L := ROUND(AdjProfitLCY_L / TotalSalesLineLCY_L.Amount * 100,0.1);
          IF SalesHeader_L."Prices Including VAT" THEN
              TotalAmount2_L := TotalSalesLine_L.Amount
          ELSE
              TotalAmount2_L := TotalSalesLine_L."Amount Including VAT";
          Amount_L += TotalAmount2_L ;
        UNTIL SalesHeader_L.NEXT=0;
      EXIT(Amount_L);
      // <-- 20130906-App29-CMC02.02.00-WSA01
    END;

    PROCEDURE IsAttachableLine@8053013(recSalesShipmentLine_P@8053001 : Record 111) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      recSalesOrderLine_L@8053000 : Record 37;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Recherche si la ligne de livraison peut ^tre attach'e . une ligne de facture
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recSalesShipmentLine_P : ligne de r'ception
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Est attachable
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //--> 20140314-APP22-CMC02.02.00-1832-FHS01
      Result_L := TRUE;
      IF recSalesOrderLine_L.GET(recSalesOrderLine_L."Document Type"::Order,
        recSalesShipmentLine_P."Order No.", recSalesShipmentLine_P."Order Line No.") THEN BEGIN
        IF ((recSalesOrderLine_L."Attached to Line No." <> 0) OR
           (recSalesOrderLine_L."RPD Item Attached Line" <> 0) OR
           (recSalesOrderLine_L."Item Charge Root Line No." <> 0)OR
           (recSalesOrderLine_L."CVO Item Attached Line" <> 0) OR
           (recSalesOrderLine_L."CVO Corn Item Attached Line" <> 0) OR
           (recSalesOrderLine_L."Free Item Root Line" <> 0)) AND
           ((recSalesOrderLine_L.Type = recSalesOrderLine_L.Type::" ") OR
           (recSalesOrderLine_L.Type = recSalesOrderLine_L.Type::"G/L Account") OR
           (recSalesOrderLine_L.Type = recSalesOrderLine_L.Type::"Charge (Item)")) THEN BEGIN
          Result_L := FALSE;
        END;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
      //<-- 20140314-APP22-CMC02.02.00-1832-FHS01
    END;

    PROCEDURE BeginReport@8053020();
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // D'but du report et initialisation
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //--> 20140324-APP22-CMC02.02.00-1896-FHS01
      // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
      // V'rification des options de traitement
      // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF G_PostingDateReq = 0D THEN
        ERROR(Text8053000);

      IF G_DocumentDateReq = 0D THEN
        ERROR(Text8053001);

      IF (G_EndingDueDateReq=0D) AND (DueDateInLine_G=TRUE) THEN
        ERROR(Text8053016);

      // --> 20140518-APP22-CMC02.02.00-2123-FEC01
      {BEFORE
      IF DueDate_G = 0D THEN
       IF NOT CONFIRM(Text8053022,FALSE) THEN
        ERROR('');
      AFTER}
      IF (DueDate_G = 0D) AND (DueDateInLine_G=TRUE) THEN
        ERROR(Text8053028);
      IF (DueDate_G <> 0D) AND (DueDate_G < G_DocumentDateReq) THEN
        ERROR(Text8053029);
      // --< 20140518-APP22-CMC02.02.00-2123-FEC01

      IF G_ActivityCodeReq = '' THEN
        IF G_AllActivity=FALSE THEN
          ERROR(Text8053017);

      G_recSalesSetup.GET;
      G_recSalesSetup.TESTFIELD("Invoice Nos.");
      G_Window.OPEN(
        //--> 20140606-APP22-CMC02.02.00-2123-FHS01
        Text8053030 +
        Text8053032 + '\' +
        //<-- 20140606-APP22-CMC02.02.00-2123-FHS01
        Text8053012 +
        Text8053013 +
        Text8053014 +
        Text8053015 + '\' +
        //--> 20140606-APP22-CMC02.02.00-2123-FHS01
        Text8053031 +
        Text8053033 + '\' +
        //<-- 20140606-APP22-CMC02.02.00-2123-FHS01
        Text8053002 +
        Text8053003 +
        Text8053004 +
        Text8053005);
      G_TotalAmount := 0;
      //<-- 20140324-APP22-CMC02.02.00-1896-FHS01
    END;

    PROCEDURE EndReport@8053019();
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Fin du report et affichage du r'sultat
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //--> 20140324-APP22-CMC02.02.00-1896-FHS01
      G_Window.CLOSE;

      IF NOT G_HideDialog THEN BEGIN

        //--> 20140324-APP22-CMC02.02.00-1896-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        {
        IF G_NoOfSalesInv > 0 THEN BEGIN
          IF (G_NoOfSalesInvErrors = 0) THEN BEGIN
            IF G_NoOfskippedShiment > 0 THEN BEGIN
              MESSAGE(Text8053010 + Text8053011, G_NoOfSalesInv,G_NoOfskippedShiment)
            END ELSE BEGIN
              MESSAGE(Text8053010, G_NoOfSalesInv);
            END;
          END ELSE
              MESSAGE(Text8053007, G_NoOfSalesInvErrors)
        END ELSE
            MESSAGE(Text8053008);
        }
        //AFTER
        //Inversion du sens d'affichage des messages
        //<-- 20140324-APP22-CMC02.02.00-1896-FHS01

        IF G_NoOfSalesCrM > 0 THEN BEGIN
          IF G_NoOfSalesCrMErrors = 0 THEN BEGIN
            // --> 20121128-Wi00-CMC02.01.00-DRN01
            //BEFORE
            //MESSAGE(Text8053020 + Text8053021, G_NoOfSalesCrM)
            //AFTER
            IF TempSalesCrMemoHeader_G.ISEMPTY THEN
            MESSAGE(Text8053020 + Text8053021, G_NoOfSalesCrM)
            ELSE BEGIN
              TempSalesCrMemoHeader_G.FINDSET;
              REPEAT
                IF SalesCrMemoHeaderList_G = '' THEN
                  SalesCrMemoHeaderList_G := TempSalesCrMemoHeader_G."No."
                ELSE
                  SalesCrMemoHeaderList_G := STRSUBSTNO('%1, %2',SalesCrMemoHeaderList_G,TempSalesCrMemoHeader_G."No.");
              UNTIL TempSalesCrMemoHeader_G.NEXT = 0;
              MESSAGE(Text8053020 + Text8053021 + Text8053024, G_NoOfSalesCrM,TempSalesCrMemoHeader_G.COUNT,SalesCrMemoHeaderList_G);
            END;
            // <-- 20121128-Wi00-CMC02.01.00-DRN01
          END ELSE
            MESSAGE(Text8053019, G_NoOfSalesCrMErrors)
        END ELSE
            MESSAGE(Text8053018);

        //--> 20140324-APP22-CMC02.02.00-1896-FHS01
        IF G_NoOfSalesInv > 0 THEN BEGIN
          IF (G_NoOfSalesInvErrors = 0) THEN BEGIN
            IF G_NoOfskippedShiment > 0 THEN BEGIN
              MESSAGE(Text8053010 + Text8053011, G_NoOfSalesInv,G_NoOfskippedShiment)
            END ELSE BEGIN
              MESSAGE(Text8053010, G_NoOfSalesInv);
            END;
          END ELSE
              MESSAGE(Text8053007, G_NoOfSalesInvErrors)
        END ELSE
            MESSAGE(Text8053008);
        //<-- 20140324-APP22-CMC02.02.00-1896-FHS01

        //--> 20140410-Cer42/001-CMC02.02.00-FHS01
        IF (NbGainMatif_G <> 0) OR (NbPrimeMatif_G <> 0) THEN BEGIN
          MESSAGE(Text8053027, NbGainMatif_G, NbPrimeMatif_G);
        END;
        //<-- 20140410-Cer42/001-CMC02.02.00-FHS01
      END;
      //<-- 20140324-APP22-CMC02.02.00-1896-FHS01
    END;

    PROCEDURE IsReturnSalesLineOK@8053021(VAR SalesLine_P@8053002 : Record 37) : Boolean;
    VAR
      IsReturnLineOK_L@8053000 : Boolean;
      IsOrderLineOK_L@8053001 : Boolean;
      SalesOrderLine_L@8053003 : Record 37;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20140407-APP20-CMC02.02.00-1939-FEC03

      IF (SalesLine_P."Price Anomaly Type" <> SalesLine_P."Price Anomaly Type"::"No Price") AND
         (ApprovalsMgt_G.CheckApprSalesLineToInvoice(SalesLine_P)) THEN
        IsReturnLineOK_L := TRUE
      ELSE
        IsReturnLineOK_L := FALSE;

      IsOrderLineOK_L := TRUE;
      IF SalesLine_P."Valued From Doc. Type" = SalesLine_P."Valued From Doc. Type"::Order THEN BEGIN
        //--> 20140410-Cer42/001-CMC02.02.00-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        //IF SalesOrderLine_L.GET(SalesLine_P."Valued From Doc. No.",SalesLine_P."Valued From Doc. Line No.") THEN BEGIN
        //AFTER
        IF SalesOrderLine_L.GET(SalesLine_P."Document Type"::"Credit Memo", SalesLine_P."Valued From Doc. No.",
                                SalesLine_P."Valued From Doc. Line No.") THEN BEGIN
        //<-- 20140410-Cer42/001-CMC02.02.00-FHS01
          IF (SalesOrderLine_L."Price Anomaly Type" <> SalesOrderLine_L."Price Anomaly Type"::"No Price") AND
             (ApprovalsMgt_G.CheckApprSalesLineToInvoice(SalesOrderLine_L)) THEN
            IsOrderLineOK_L := TRUE
          ELSE
            IsOrderLineOK_L := FALSE;
        END;
      END;

      EXIT(IsReturnLineOK_L AND IsOrderLineOK_L);

      // --< 20140407-APP20-CMC02.02.00-1939-FEC03
    END;

  <Code>Public Function BlankZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value = 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankZeroAndPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNeg(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNegAndZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function
